- **a-stock-diagnosis**: A股个股深度技术诊断工具。基于60日K线数据计算MA5/10/20/60均线、量价关系、 支撑压力位和风险信号，由 Agent 进行多维分析并输出结构化诊断报告。 Use when: (1) 用户说"帮我分析下这只股票""诊断下600519", (2) 用户问"XX技术面怎么样""趋势如何", (3) 用户要求"个股诊断""综合评估""支撑位在哪", (4) 用户要求多只股票对比分析。 Distinct from: a-stock-realtime (实时行情快查+分时量能+持仓管理), market-briefing (宏观财经新闻简报)。 Do NOT trigger: 用户只查当前价格（用 a-stock-realtime）, 用户要求量化回测或策略编写, 用户查基金/港美股/期货（本技能仅支持A股）。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/qwenwork/skills/a-stock-diagnosis) · ★103 · 2 copies)

- **a-stock-lhb-rec**: 龙虎榜「后期涨停」个股推荐与自进化模型技能。基于近三个月龙虎榜数据训练逻辑回归模型， 预测个股「上榜后 T+1/T+2/T+3 任一交易日涨停」的概率，收盘后（17:00 后）生成推荐候选， 并据「昨日龙虎榜+今日涨停+今日龙虎榜」持续自我验证与重训，让模型随真实结果累积越来越精准。 特征三维：个股技术面（涨幅/换手/市值/净买/是否涨停/前3日透支/上榜原因）、席位操作手法 （买方质量/家数/买卖净比/游资占比/机构/北向/知名游资/低吸）、行情研判 （题材主线/板块强度/市场涨停情绪/连板强势）。含游资席位画像、紫阳东路专项、模型进化轨迹。 适用：盘后龙虎榜选股、游资后期涨停预判、T+3 买入候选、席位手法分析、模型自迭代优化。 ([smart-open/skills](https://github.com/smart-open/skills/tree/HEAD/a-stock-lhb-rec) · ★23)

- **a-stock-prediction**: A股个股次日(T+1)走势预测 skill。当用户输入A股股票代码(如 600519、000858、300750、688981、430090)或公司名称(如"贵州茅台"、"宁德时代"、"比亚迪"),并要求预测次日走势、给出交易计划、评估盈亏概率时,触发本 skill。skill 会用 mcp__MiniMax__web_search 搜证大盘环境、龙虎榜、北向资金、融资融券、主力资金流、板块联动、公告与消息面等,按"四维分析框架"(大盘环境 30% / 资金行为 30% / 技术面 25% / 消息面 15%)交叉验证,产出可量化的方向预测(看多/看空/震荡 + 概率百分比 + 价格区间),并写入当前项目 markdown 目录下的 `a-stock-{代码}-{YYYY-MM-DD}.md`。输出包含详细逻辑推导链、中间结果与最终结果概率、专业术语小白解释、mermaid/ascii/svg 图表。适用场景:盘后复盘想做次日的方向判断与交易计划;不适用:长线基本面估值(改用 finance-core-analysis)、日内高频交易、港股/美股(改用 us-stock-prediction)。 ([digoal/blog](https://github.com/digoal/blog/tree/HEAD/skills/a-stock-prediction) · ★8.6k · 2 copies)

- **a11y-focus-audit**: 프론트엔드 파일이나 화면의 접근성 코드 품질과 실제 키보드 Tab 포커스 이동을 점검한다. 트리거 — "접근성 확인해줘", "탭 포커스 이동 확인해줘", "a11y 리뷰해줘", "키보드 접근성 봐줘". 비-트리거: 디자인 픽셀 퍼펙트 비교만 원하는 요청; 서버/API 접근성 무관 코드 리뷰; 실제 수정 구현만 요청하고 접근성 검토를 원하지 않는 경우. ([oww1220/codex-toolkit](https://github.com/oww1220/codex-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/frontend-tools/skills/a11y-focus-audit) · ★1)
- **a11y-focus-management**: Use when building a modal, dialog, or any UI that changes what's focused programmatically — moving focus in on open and restoring it on close. Use whenever a component conditionally renders/removes focusable content based on state (isOpen, route change). ([arayaroma/ether](https://github.com/arayaroma/ether/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-focus-management))
- **a11y-focus-visibility-under-sticky-header**: Audits and verifies a11y focus visibility under sticky header with explicit acceptance checks and recoverable failure handling. Use when implementation, configuration, release, or incident evidence must be reviewed for this boundary. ([Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code](https://github.com/Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code/tree/HEAD/curated-skill-sources/accessibility-inclusive-ux/a11y-focus-visibility-under-sticky-header) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **a11y-form-patterns**: &gt; Build accessible forms with proper labeling, grouped controls, inline validation, and clear error communication ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/a11y-form-patterns) · ★23 · 4 copies)

- **a11y-forms**: Accessible form patterns. Activate when writing or editing HTML forms, `&lt;input&gt;`, `&lt;select&gt;`, `&lt;textarea&gt;`, or form validation logic — covers labelling, input types, autocomplete, and error announcements. ([TheNordicOne/ngx-formbar](https://github.com/TheNordicOne/ngx-formbar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/a11y-forms) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **a11y-forms**: Reference guide for accessible forms — labels, descriptions, error identification and validation messaging, required-field semantics, autocomplete, and grouping, including Fluent UI v9 Field and Label patterns. Use when building or reviewing form UI, or when asked how to make inputs, selects, or validation accessible. ([aidevme/a11y-skills](https://github.com/aidevme/a11y-skills/tree/HEAD/.github/plugins/a11y/skills/a11y-forms))
- **a11y-forms**: Use when building or reviewing any form input, select, or textarea in React/Next.js — label connection via htmlFor/id, required-field signaling, and error-message wiring via aria-describedby/aria-invalid. The most commonly flagged a11y issue in code review. ([arayaroma/ether](https://github.com/arayaroma/ether/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-forms))
- **a11y-forms**: Audits form inputs, labels, fieldsets, validation errors, autocomplete attributes, and multi-step wizards. Use when reviewing forms or interactive input controls in Phase 4. ([porsche-design-system/testing](https://github.com/porsche-design-system/testing/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/a11y-forms))
- **a11y-forms-errors**: Build accessible forms: visible labels, hints, required state, autocomplete tokens, fieldsets, inline errors tied to fields, error summaries and focus, WCAG 2.2 redundant entry and accessible authentication. Use when creating or reviewing forms, sign-in, checkout or validation. ([posterus-ltd/yarkr-public](https://github.com/posterus-ltd/yarkr-public/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-forms-errors))
- **a11y-framework**: Provides framework-specific accessibility patterns and fix templates for React, Next.js, Vue, Angular, Svelte, and Tailwind CSS. Use during Phase 0 stack discovery or when generating framework-specific remediation snippets. ([porsche-design-system/testing](https://github.com/porsche-design-system/testing/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/a11y-framework))
- **a11y-gate**: Statically verify new UI primitives for accessibility — animations gated behind prefers-reduced-motion, keyboard accessible, ARIA present — before declaring the primitive done. Use when authoring or modifying a UI primitive (component, page section, interactive widget), especially interactive/animated ones. Pairs well as a fill-the-wait task while builds run. ([jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills](https://github.com/jcdavis131/cursor-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-gate))
- **a11y-gate**: Audit and fix web accessibility to WCAG 2.2 AA, gated by automated checks that actually run — axe-core via Playwright for violations, scripted keyboard/focus and reflow audits, plus a mandatory manual + screen-reader checklist. Use when the user wants to make a site/app/page accessible, fix a11y or WCAG/ADA/Section 508 issues, add an accessibility CI gate, run an axe/Lighthouse-style audit, check keyboard navigation, color contrast, ARIA, screen-reader support, or remediate accessibility violations. Triggers: "accessibility", "a11y", "WCAG", "ADA compliance", "screen reader", "keyboard naviga… ([NeuralMedic-DE/claude-skills](https://github.com/NeuralMedic-DE/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/a11y-gate))
- **a11y-gate**: Set up or maintain an automated accessibility (axe-core) test gate with a baseline, so CI blocks new critical/serious violations without requiring existing debt to be fixed first. Use whenever asked to add an accessibility gate, wire up axe scanning, or update an accessibility baseline. ([timainge/copilot-powers](https://github.com/timainge/copilot-powers/tree/HEAD/playwright-a11y/skills/a11y-gate))
- **a11y-gate**: Checks a UI change against WCAG 2.2 AA before it ships: automated scans plus keyboard, screen reader, contrast, zoom, motion and touch checks on every changed screen and state, with a pass or fail verdict. Use when a screen, page, component or flow is ready to release. ([zeusstudiodev/worklings](https://github.com/zeusstudiodev/worklings/tree/HEAD/library/skills/a11y-gate) · 3 copies)
- **a11y-gauntlet**: Accessibility validator for any web or app project: plain HTML and vanilla JS, and any framework (React, Next, Vue, Nuxt, Svelte, Angular, Astro, Lit, Alpine, htmx, server templates such as Blade, Razor, Twig, Django), plus SwiftUI, Android and React Native. Audits code (and the running app when possible) against WCAG 2.2 level A and AA plus a widget behaviour contract (roles, ARIA states, keyboard map, focus management, accessible names) for dialogs, menus, selects, comboboxes, tabs, accordions, sliders, switches, toasts and forms. Reports findings as a Before/After/Why table with severity,… ([diogotl/a11y-gauntlet](https://github.com/diogotl/a11y-gauntlet/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **a11y-generator**: Use esta skill sempre que componentes Angular de UI (Presenters, templates HTML, SCSS) estiverem sendo criados, avaliados ou refatorados. Garante acessibilidade nativa WCAG 2.1/2.2 Nível AA/AAA, navegação por teclado e conformidade WAI-ARIA em todo componente visual. ([joaoGuiotti/jg-multi-brand-retail](https://github.com/joaoGuiotti/jg-multi-brand-retail/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/a11y-generator))
- **a11y-grid**: Guides accessible data grid, interactive table, and sortable table implementation. Auto-invokes when creating data grids, spreadsheet interfaces, sortable tables, or interactive table cells. Critical distinction — most data tables do NOT need role="grid". Covers table vs grid decision, HTML table accessibility, sortable columns, and the APG grid pattern. ([xrnavigation/web-a11y-plugin](https://github.com/xrnavigation/web-a11y-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/a11y/skills/a11y-grid) · ★1)

- **a11y-guardian**: Continuous accessibility monitoring. Runs WCAG 2.1 audit on every PR, blocks merge on CRITICAL violations, and posts inline PR comments with fixes. Use when setting up CI accessibility gates or reviewing PR for a11y regressions. ([Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub](https://github.com/Andersonlimahw/lemon-ai-hub/tree/HEAD/plugins/a11y-guardian) · ★18 · 2 copies)
- **a11y-guide**: Accessibility guide for Mobazha following WCAG 2.1 AA standards including color contrast, reduced motion, ARIA patterns, keyboard navigation, focus management, and screen reader testing. Use when building accessible components or reviewing accessibility, "无障碍", "可访问性", "a11y", "ARIA", "键盘导航", "屏幕阅读器", "对比度". ([mobazha/mobazha-unified](https://github.com/mobazha/mobazha-unified/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/a11y-guide) · ★123)
- **a11y-hiding-and-tables-pitfalls**: Use when content has to be hidden from sighted users but kept for screen readers (a label for an icon-only control, a skip link), hidden from assistive technology only, or hidden from everyone, and when a data table is marked up: the clip pattern for visually hidden text, making a hidden focusable element appear on focus, display none versus aria-hidden versus the hidden attribute, table captions, and th cells with scope for column and row headers. The general semantics, ARIA and keyboard rules are accessibility. ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-hiding-and-tables-pitfalls))
- **a11y-high-contrast-theme-rendering-test**: Audits and verifies a11y high contrast theme rendering test with explicit acceptance checks and recoverable failure handling. - **a11y-image-text-alt**: &gt; Write effective alt text for images and provide text alternatives for all non-text content ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/a11y-image-text-alt) · ★23 · 4 copies)
- **a11y-implementation-guide**: Implement accessibility. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with a11y-implementation-guide related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-implementation-guide) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **a11y-init**: Sets up accessibility governance in a project. ([aidevme/a11y-skills](https://github.com/aidevme/a11y-skills/tree/HEAD/.github/plugins/a11y/skills/a11y-init))
- **a11y-interactive**: Accessible interactive patterns. ([TheNordicOne/ngx-formbar](https://github.com/TheNordicOne/ngx-formbar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/a11y-interactive) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **a11y-issue-fixer**: Applies auto-fixable and guided accessibility fixes to web source code from an audit report and coordinates post-fix verification. - **a11y-issue-writer**: Format accessibility violations into standardized, JIRA-ready issue reports using the accessibility-issues-template-mcp. Triggers on requests like "write issues for these violations", "format as JIRA tickets", "create accessibility bug reports", or "generate issue from axe results". ([joe-watkins/a11y-devkit](https://github.com/joe-watkins/a11y-devkit/tree/HEAD/packages/a11y-issue-writer) · ★1)
- **a11y-judgment**: Use when auditing dialed.run's screens for accessibility problems that need judgment, not a rule — whether names, headings, errors and reading order make sense when heard — or when reviewing a PR's screen-reader transcript diff. Covers the criteria, the full audit's fan-out over `e2e/transcripts/`, and the finding format. ([ddteeter/dialed.run](https://github.com/ddteeter/dialed.run/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/a11y-judgment))
- **a11y-judgment**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "check accessibility", "audit a11y", "run an accessibility review", "check WCAG compliance", "is this accessible", "review alt text", "check screen reader support", or works on UI code where accessibility quality matters. Covers the WCAG criteria that automated tools (axe-core, Lighthouse, eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y) cannot check because they require judgment about meaning. ([jagansanikommu/a11y-judgment](https://github.com/jagansanikommu/a11y-judgment/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-judgment))
- **a11y-keyboard**: Reference guide for keyboard accessibility — tab order, focus visibility, roving tabindex, skip links, and keyboard operability of custom widgets. Use when building or reviewing interactive components, or when asked about keyboard navigation, focus management, or tabindex. ([aidevme/a11y-skills](https://github.com/aidevme/a11y-skills/tree/HEAD/.github/plugins/a11y/skills/a11y-keyboard))
- **a11y-keyboard**: Reviews keyboard navigation, tab order, focus management, roving tabindex, skip links, and shortcut conflicts. Use when auditing keyboard operability and focus behavior in Phase 3. ([porsche-design-system/testing](https://github.com/porsche-design-system/testing/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/a11y-keyboard))
- **a11y-keyboard-focus**: Make UIs fully keyboard operable: logical tab order, visible and unobscured focus, skip links, roving tabindex, modal focus traps, focus on SPA route changes (WCAG 2.1.1, 2.4.3, 2.4.7, 2.4.11). Use when building interactive components or fixing lost or invisible focus. ([posterus-ltd/yarkr-public](https://github.com/posterus-ltd/yarkr-public/tree/HEAD/skills/a11y-keyboard-focus))
- **a11y-keyboard-nav**: Use when building a custom interactive widget in React that isn't a native input/button/link (dropdown, combobox, custom menu) — keyboard handlers for arrow keys, Enter/Space, Escape. - **a11y-keyboard-navigation**: &gt; Ensure all interactive elements are reachable and operable via keyboard alone without requiring a mouse ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/a11y-keyboard-navigation) · ★23 · 4 copies)

- **a11y-label-audit**: 扫描 React/TSX 组件中缺失的无障碍标签并自动修复。 检测纯图标按钮缺少 aria-label、div/span 模拟按钮缺少 role 和键盘交互、 图片缺少有意义的 alt 文本、表单元素缺少 label 关联、 弹窗缺少 aria-modal 和焦点管理、动态内容缺少 aria-live 等问题。 同时考虑跨平台兼容性问题：Tauri 桌面端（WKWebView/WebView2）、 微信/QQ 内置浏览器、Chrome 扩展、受限 WebView 等不同引擎对 ARIA 属性和焦点管理的支持差异。 当用户提到无障碍审查、a11y 检查、WCAG 合规、添加 aria 标签、 屏幕阅读器支持、无障碍改进、兼容性问题时使用此技能。 ([Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/RA-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/community/clawhub/a/a11y-label-audit) · ★10)

- **a11y-lint-wiring-pitfalls**: Use when accessibility lint rules are added to a JSX or Vue project or a lint run is quiet for the wrong reason: wrapper components the rules cannot see until they are declared, polymorphic components, label rules and their component and depth options, flat-config scoping and globals, presets that leave rules off, label association by nesting versus for and id, deterministic ids, anchors that act as buttons, click handlers without key handlers, autofocus, and deprecated rules not to enable. - **a11y-listbox**: Guides accessible listbox implementation for single and multi-select lists per APG patterns.
